30396:   function buildTree(ds){var by={},root=[];(ds||[]).forEach(function(x){by[x.id]={id:x.id,name:x.name,code:x.code,parent:x.parent_department_id,manager:x.manager_employee_id,children:[]}});Object.keys(by).forEach(function(k){var x=by[k];if(x.parent&&by[x.parent])by[x.parent].children.push(x);else root.push(x)});function node(x,depth){var manager=H.employees.filter(function(e){return e.id===x.manager})[0];return '<div class="mr-'+Math.min(depth*3,12)+' rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 p-4 bg-white shadow-sm"><div class="flex justify-between gap-3"><div><div class="font-black">'+esc(x.name)+'</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500">'+esc(x.code||'-')+(manager?' · مدير: '+esc(manager.name):'')+'</div></div>'+badge(x.children.length+' فرعي','info')+'</div>'+(x.children.length?'<div class="mt-3 space-y-3 border-r-2 border-slate-100 pr-4">'+x.children.map(function(c){return node(c,depth+1)}).join('')+'</div>':'')+'</div>'}return root.map(function(x){return node(x,0)}).join('')||'<div class="py-10 text-center text-slate-400 font-bold">لم تُنشأ إدارات بعد</div>'}

30456:     }catch(e){}

30383: function closeModal(){var r=E('rw-hr-modal-root');if(r)r.remove()}
30384:   function ppl(){return H.employees.filter(function(e){return String(e.role||'').toLowerCase()!=='owner'&&e.role!=='مالك'}).map(function(e){return{value:e.id,label:(e.name||e.email)+' — '+e.email}})}
30385:   async function loadPeople(){var d=await q('employees');H.employees=d.rows||[];return H.employees}
30386:   async function loadBranches(){var r=await supabase.from('branches').select('id,branch_code,name,is_active').eq('company_id',H.companyId).order('name');if(r.error)throw r.error;H.branches=r.data||[];return H.branches}
30387:   function branches(){return H.branches.filter(function(x){return x.is_active!==false}).map(function(x){return{value:x.id,label:(x.branch_code||'')+' — '+x.name}})}
30388:   function employeeOpts(){return ppl()}
30389:   function deptOpts(rows){return (rows||[]).map(function(x){return{value:x.id,label:x.name}})}
30390:   function posOpts(rows){return (rows||[]).map(function(x){return{value:x.id,label:x.title}})}
30391:   function scheduleOpts(rows){return (rows||[]).map(function(x){return{value:x.id,label:x.name}})}
30392:   function tabbar(){return '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 shadow-sm p-2 flex gap-2 flex-wrap">'+T.map(function(x){return '<button type="button" data-hr-tab="'+x[0]+'" class="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl font-black text-sm '+(H.tab===x[0]?'bg-indigo-600 text-white':'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50')+'"><i class="fas '+x[2]+' ml-1"></i>'+x[1]+'</button>'}).join('')+'</div>'}
30393:   function employeeMeta(e){return '<div class="space-y-2 text-sm"><div><span class="text-slate-500">القسم:</span> <b>'+esc(e.department_name||e.department||'-')+'</b></div><div><span class="text-slate-500">الوظيفة:</span> <b>'+esc(e.position_name||e.job_title||e.role||'-')+'</b></div><div><span class="text-slate-500">الفرع:</span> <b>'+esc(e.branch_name||'-')+'</b></div><div><span class="text-slate-500">العقد:</span> '+(e.contract_status==='active'?badge('فعال','ok'):badge(e.contract_status||'غير موجود','muted'))+'</div></div>'}

30432:   (function installModalResilience(){
30433:     document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
30434:       var ac=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('[data-hr-action]');
30435:       if(!ac)return;
30436:       var page=E('rw-page-container');
30437:       if(page&&page.contains(ac))return;
30438:       e.preventDefault();
30439:       handle(ac.getAttribute('data-hr-action'));
30440:     },true);
30441:     window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',function(e){
30442:       var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
30443:       if(!root)return;
30444:       e.preventDefault();
30445:       var msg=e.reason&&(e.reason.message||String(e.reason));
30446:       if(msg)toast(msg,'error');
30447:     });
30448:     try{
30449:       var mo=new MutationObserver(function(){
30450:         var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
30451:         if(!root||!E('hr360'))return;
30452:         var f=E('rw-hr-form');
30453:         if(f&&f.lastElementChild)f.lastElementChild.style.display='none';
30454:       });
30455:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
30456:     }catch(e){}
30457:   }());

28067: function _exportReportCsv() {
28068:     var resultDiv = byId('report-result');
28069: 
28070:     if (!resultDiv) {
28071:         _showToast('لا يوجد تقرير للتصدير', 'info');
28072:         return;
28073:     }
28074: 
28075:     var table = resultDiv.querySelector('table');
28076: 
28077:     if (!table) {
28078:         _showToast('لا يوجد جدول قابل للتصدير', 'info');
28079:         return;
28080:     }
28081: 
28082:     var rows = table.querySelectorAll('tr');
28083:     var csv = [];
28084: 
28085:     function csvCell(value) {
28086:         var s = String(value == null ? '' : value)
28087:             .replace(/\r?\n|\r/g, ' ')
28088:             .replace(/"/g, '""');
28089: 
28090:         return '"' + s + '"';
28091:     }
28092: 
28093:     for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
28094:         var cells = rows[i].querySelectorAll('th,td');
28095:         var line = [];
28096: 
28097:         for (var j = 0; j < cells.length; j++) {
28098:             line.push(csvCell(cells[j].innerText || ''));
28099:         }
28100: 
28101:         csv.push(line.join(','));
28102:     }
28103: 
28104:     var blob = new Blob(
28105:         ['\uFEFF' + csv.join('\r\n')],
28106:         { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' }
28107:     );
28108: 
28109:     var url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
28110:     var a = document.createElement('a');
28111: 
28112:     a.href = url;
28113:     a.download =
28114:         'rawaea-report-' +
28115:         String(_currentReport || 'report') +
28116:         '-' +
28117:         new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10) +
28118:         '.csv';
28119: 
28120:     document.body.appendChild(a);
28121:     a.click();
28122:     document.body.removeChild(a);
28123: 
28124:     URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
28125: }
28126: function _printReport() {
28127:     var resultDiv = byId('report-result');
28128: 
28129:     if (!resultDiv || !resultDiv.innerHTML) {
28130:         _showToast('لا يوجد تقرير للطباعة', 'info');
28131:         return;
28132:     }
28133: 
28134:     var printWindow = window.open('', '_blank');
28135: 
28136:     if (!printWindow) {
28137:         _showToast('الرجاء السماح بالنوافذ المنبثقة', 'warning');
28138:         return;
28139:     }
28140: 
28141:     var html =
28142:         '<!DOCTYPE html>' +
28143:         '<html dir="rtl">' +
28144:         '<head>' +
28145:         '<meta charset="UTF-8">' +
28146:         '<title>تقرير الروائع ERP</title>' +
28147:         '<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Cairo:wght@400;700;900&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">' +
28148:         '<style>' +
28149:         'body{font-family:Cairo,sans-serif;padding:20px;color:#111827}' +
28150:         'table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;margin-top:15px}' +
28151:         'th,td{border:1px solid #ddd;padding:8px}' +
28152:         'th{background:#f2f2f2;font-weight:800}' +
28153:         '.text-center{text-align:center}' +
28154:         '</style>' +
28155:         '</head>' +
28156:         '<body>' +
28157:         resultDiv.innerHTML +
28158:         '<script>window.onload=function(){window.print();};<\/script>' +
28159:         '</body></html>';
28160: 
28161:     printWindow.document.open();
28162:     printWindow.document.write(html);
28163:     printWindow.document.close();
28164: }

30314: var RW_HR = (function() {

30320:     ['requests','الطلبات','fa-list-check'],['advances','السلف','fa-hand-holding-dollar'],['payroll','الرواتب','fa-money-check-dollar'],['documents','المستندات','fa-folder-open']
30321:   ];
30322:   function E(id){return typeof byId==='function'?byId(id):document.getElementById(id)}
30323:   function esc(v){return String(v==null?'':v).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/\"/g,'&quot;').replace(/'/g,'&#39;')}
30324:   function num(v){v=Number(v);return isFinite(v)?v:0}
30325:   function money(v){return num(v).toLocaleString('ar-EG',{maximumFractionDigits:2})}
30326:   function date(v){return v?String(v).slice(0,10).split('-').reverse().join('/'):'-'}
30327:   function iso(v){return v?new Date(v).toISOString():null}
30328:   function toast(m,k){if(typeof showToast==='function')return showToast(m,k||'success');if(typeof Swal!=='undefined')return Swal.fire({toast:true,position:'top-end',icon:k||'success',title:m,showConfirmButton:false,timer:2600});alert(m)}
30329:   function safe(el,html){if(!el)return;if(typeof safeHTML==='function')safeHTML(el,html);else el.innerHTML=html}
30330:   function opKey(k){if(!H.ops[k])H.ops[k]='MOTHER-HR:'+k+':'+Date.now()+':'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,10);return H.ops[k]}
30331:   function opClear(k){if(k)delete H.ops[k]}
30332:   async function actor(){var a=await supabase.auth.getUser();if(a.error||!a.data.user)throw Error('جلسة المستخدم غير صالحة');var u=await supabase.from('users').select('id,email,company_id,role,name,status,phone,employee_id,default_branch_id,active_warehouse_role').eq('auth_id',a.data.user.id).maybeSingle();if(u.error)throw u.error;if(!u.data||!u.data.id||!u.data.company_id)throw Error('تعذر تحديد سياق الموظف والشركة');H.actor=u.data;H.companyId=u.data.company_id}
30333:   async function q(view,payload){var r=await supabase.rpc('hr_query',{p_view:view,p_payload:payload||{}});if(r.error)throw r.error;if(!r.data||r.data.success===false)throw Error((r.data&&(r.data.msg||r.data.code))||'فشل قراءة HR');return r.data}
30334:   async function c(command,payload,key){var k=key||('cmd:'+command);var r=await supabase.rpc('hr_command_atomic',{p_command:command,p_payload:payload||{},p_operation_id:opKey(k),p_actor_user_id:H.actor.id,p_actor_email:H.actor.email});if(r.error)throw r.error;if(!r.data||r.data.success===false)throw Error((r.data&&(r.data.msg||r.data.code))||'فشل تنفيذ أمر HR');opClear(k);return r.data}
30335:   function btn(text,action,cls){return '<button type="button" data-hr-action="'+esc(action)+'" class="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl font-black text-sm '+(cls||'bg-indigo-600 text-white hover:bg-indigo-700')+'">'+esc(text)+'</button>'}
30336:   function badge(text,k){var m={ok:'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-100',warn:'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-100',bad:'bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border-rose-100',info:'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border-blue-100',muted:'bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 border-slate-100'};return '<span class="inline-flex items-center px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full border text-[11px] font-black '+(m[k]||m.muted)+'">'+esc(text)+'</span>'}
30337:   function card(title,sub,body,actions){return '<section class="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-slate-100 shadow-sm overflow-hidden"><div class="px-6 py-5 bg-slate-50/80 border-b flex flex-col lg:flex-row lg:items-center justify-between gap-3"><div><h3 class="font-black text-slate-800">'+esc(title)+'</h3><p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">'+esc(sub||'')+'</p></div><div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">'+(actions||'')+'</div></div><div class="p-6">'+body+'</div></section>'}
30338:   function stat(title,value,icon,cls){return '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 p-5 shadow-sm"><div class="flex items-center justify-between"><div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500 font-bold">'+esc(title)+'</div><div class="text-2xl font-black mt-2">'+esc(value)+'</div></div><div class="w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center '+(cls||'bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700')+'"><i class="fas '+icon+'"></i></div></div></div>'}
30339:   function table(headers,rows){if(!rows||!rows.length)return '<div class="py-10 text-center text-slate-400 font-bold">لا توجد بيانات</div>';return '<div class="overflow-auto"><table class="min-w-full text-sm"><thead><tr>'+headers.map(function(h){return '<th class="px-4 py-3 text-right bg-slate-50 text-slate-500 font-black whitespace-nowrap">'+esc(h)+'</th>'}).join('')+'</tr></thead><tbody>'+rows.join('')+'</tbody></table></div>'}

30343:   function select(label,id,list,value){return '<label class="block"><span class="block text-xs font-black text-slate-600 mb-2">'+esc(label)+'</span><select id="'+esc(id)+'" class="w-full px-4 py-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-200">'+(list||[]).map(function(x){return '<option value="'+esc(x.value)+'"'+(String(x.value)===String(value==null?'':value)?' selected':'')+'>'+esc(x.label)+'</option>'}).join('')+'</select></label>'}

30431:   // Resilience layer: modal actions work outside the page-container, async form errors become visible, and 360 is truly read-only.
30432:   (function installModalResilience(){
30433:     document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
30434:       var ac=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('[data-hr-action]');
30435:       if(!ac)return;
30436:       var page=E('rw-page-container');
30437:       if(page&&page.contains(ac))return;
30438:       e.preventDefault();
30439:       handle(ac.getAttribute('data-hr-action'));
30440:     },true);
30441:     window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',function(e){
30442:       var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
30443:       if(!root)return;
30444:       e.preventDefault();
30445:       var msg=e.reason&&(e.reason.message||String(e.reason));
30446:       if(msg)toast(msg,'error');
30447:     });
30448:     try{
30449:       var mo=new MutationObserver(function(){
30450:         var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
30451:         if(!root||!E('hr360'))return;
30452:         var f=E('rw-hr-form');
30453:         if(f&&f.lastElementChild)f.lastElementChild.style.display='none';
30454:       });
30455:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
30456:     }catch(e){}
30457:   }());

30479:             .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
30480:             .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
30481:             .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
30482:             .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')

30491:         return Number(n || 0).toLocaleString('ar-EG') + ' EGP';
30492:     }

30495:         var d = new Date();
30496:         var local = new Date(d.getTime() - d.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000);

30498:     }

30465: var RW_CRM = (function() {
30466:     'use strict';
30467: 
30468:     var state = {
30469:         customers: [],
30470:         assignees: [],
30471:         kpi: {},
30472:         search: '',
30473:         activeOnly: false,
30474:         searchTimer: null
30475:     };
30476: 
30477:     function _esc(s) {
30478:         return String(s == null ? '' : s)
30479:             .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
30480:             .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
30481:             .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
30482:             .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
30483:             .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
30484:     }
30485: 
30486:     function _fmtNum(n) {
30487:         return Number(n || 0).toLocaleString('ar-EG');
30488:     }
30489: 
30490:     function _fmtMoney(n) {
30491:         return Number(n || 0).toLocaleString('ar-EG') + ' EGP';
30492:     }
30493: 
30494:     function _today() {
30495:         var d = new Date();
30496:         var local = new Date(d.getTime() - d.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000);
30497:         return local.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
30498:     }
30499: 
30500:     function _statusLabel(s) {
30501:         var map = {
30502:             Open: 'مفتوحة',
30503:             'معلقة': 'معلقة',
30504:             completed: 'مكتملة',
30505:             'مكتملة': 'مكتملة',
30506:             cancelled: 'ملغاة',
30507:             'ملغاة': 'ملغاة'
30508:         };
30509:         return map[s] || s || 'غير محددة';
30510:     }
30511: 
30512:     function _statusClass(s) {
30513:         if (s === 'completed' || s === 'مكتملة') return 'bg-green-100 text-green-700';
30514:         if (s === 'cancelled' || s === 'ملغاة') return 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600';
30515:         return 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700';
30516:     }
30517: 
30518:     function _assignedLabel(customer) {
30519:         var rows = Array.isArray(customer && customer.assigned_to) ? customer.assigned_to : [];
30520:         if (!rows.length) return 'غير مسند';
30521:         var active = rows.filter(function(x) { return x && x.active !== false; });
30522:         if (!active.length) active = rows;
30523:         return active.slice(0, 2).map(function(x) {
30524:             return x.name || x.email || '—';
30525:         }).join('، ') + (active.length > 2 ? ' +' + (active.length - 2) : '');
30526:     }
30527: 
30528:     async function _loadDirectory() {
30529:         var res = await supabase.rpc('crm_customer_directory', {
30530:             p_search: state.search || null,
30531:             p_active_only: state.activeOnly,
30532:             p_limit: 200,
30533:             p_offset: 0
30534:         });
30535:         if (res.error) throw res.error;
30536: 
30537:         var payload = res.data || {};
30538:         state.customers = Array.isArray(payload.customers) ? payload.customers : [];
30539:         state.assignees = Array.isArray(payload.assignees) ? payload.assignees : [];
30540:         state.kpi = payload.kpi || {};
30541:         return payload;
30542:     }
30543: 
30544:     function _renderKpis() {
30545:         var k = state.kpi || {};
30546:         return '<div class="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-6 gap-4">' +
30547:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">إجمالي العملاء</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-indigo-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.total_customers) + '</div></div>' +
30548:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">عملاء نشطون</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-green-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.active_customers) + '</div></div>' +
30549:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">ذمم مسجلة</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-red-600 mt-2">' + _fmtMoney(k.master_debt_total) + '</div></div>' +
30550:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">متابعات مفتوحة</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-amber-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.open_followups) + '</div></div>' +
30551:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">متأخرة</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-rose-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.overdue_followups) + '</div></div>' +
30552:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">مستحقة اليوم</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-blue-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.due_today) + '</div></div>' +
30553:         '</div>';
30554:     }
30555: 
30556:     function _renderTable() {
30557:         if (!state.customers.length) {
30558:             return '<div class="text-center py-16 text-gray-400"><div class="text-5xl mb-3">👥</div><div class="font-black text-lg">لا توجد عملاء مطابقون</div><div class="text-sm mt-2">غيّر البحث أو الفلاتر ثم أعد المحاولة.</div></div>';
30559:         }
30560: 
30561:         var html = '<div class="overflow-x-auto"><table class="w-full text-sm">' +
30562:             '<thead class="bg-slate-50"><tr>' +
30563:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">العميل</th>' +
30564:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">التواصل</th>' +
30565:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">التصنيف</th>' +
30566:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">المبيعات</th>' +
30567:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">الأوردرات</th>' +
30568:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">المتابعة القادمة</th>' +
30569:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">المسؤول</th>' +
30570:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">الإجراء</th>' +
30571:             '</tr></thead><tbody>';
30572: 
30573:         for (var i = 0; i < state.customers.length; i++) {
30574:             var c = state.customers[i] || {};
30575:             var overdue = Number(c.overdue_followups || 0) > 0;
30576:             var next = c.next_followup_date ? String(c.next_followup_date) : '—';
30577: 
30578:             html += '<tr class="border-b hover:bg-slate-50">' +
30579:                 '<td class="p-3"><div class="font-black">' + _esc(c.name) + '</div><div class="text-xs text-gray-400">' + _esc(c.customer_code) + '</div></td>' +
30580:                 '<td class="p-3"><div>' + _esc(c.phone || '—') + '</div><div class="text-xs text-gray-400">' + _esc(c.area || '—') + '</div></td>' +
30581:                 '<td class="p-3"><div class="font-bold">' + _esc(c.customer_type || '—') + '</div><div class="text-xs text-gray-400">' + _esc(c.payment_type || '—') + '</div></td>' +
30582:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center font-black">' + _fmtMoney(c.sales_total) + '</td>' +
30583:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center font-black">' + _fmtNum(c.order_count) + '</td>' +
30584:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center"><span class="px-2 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-black ' + (overdue ? 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-700' : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700') + '">' + _esc(next) + '</span></td>' +
30585:                 '<td class="p-3">' + _esc(_assignedLabel(c)) + '</td>' +
30586:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center"><button type="button" data-crm-open360="' + _esc(c.id) + '" class="px-4 py-2 bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded-xl font-black">متابعة</button></td>' +
30587:             '</tr>';
30588:         }
30589: 
30590:         return html + '</tbody></table></div>';
30591:     }
30592: 
30593:     function _bindDirectory() {
30594:         var search = byId('crm-search');
30595:         if (search) {
30596:             search.value = state.search;
30597:             search.addEventListener('input', function() {
30598:                 state.search = search.value.trim();
30599:                 clearTimeout(state.searchTimer);
30600:                 state.searchTimer = setTimeout(function() {
30601:                     _loadDirectory().then(function() {
30602:                         safeHTML(byId('crm-customers-list'), _renderTable());
30603:                         _bindOpen360();
30604:                         safeHTML(byId('crm-kpis'), _renderKpis());
30605:                     }).catch(function(e) {
30606:                         showToast(e.message || 'فشل البحث', 'error');
30607:                     });
30608:                 }, 250);
30609:             });
30610:         }

30430:   function realtime(){try{if(H.channel)supabase.removeChannel(H.channel);var tables=['employee_profiles','employee_attendance','employee_leave_requests','employee_documents','hr_departments','hr_positions','hr_employee_assignments','hr_employee_schedule_assignments','hr_work_schedules','hr_attendance_events','hr_work_entries','hr_leave_types','hr_leave_balances','hr_requests','hr_request_approvals','hr_salary_advances','hr_salary_components','hr_contracts','hr_contract_components','hr_payroll_periods','hr_payroll_runs','hr_payslips','hr_payslip_lines','hr_payroll_accounting_map'];H.channel=supabase.channel('rw-hr-mother-final');tables.forEach(function(t){H.channel.on('postgres_changes',{event:'*',schema:'public',table:t},function(){clearTimeout(H.timer);H.timer=setTimeout(function(){render()},700)})});H.channel.subscribe()}catch(e){console.warn('RW_HR realtime',e)}}
30431:   // Resilience layer: modal actions work outside the page-container, async form errors become visible, and 360 is truly read-only.
30432:   (function installModalResilience(){
30433:     document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
30434:       var ac=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('[data-hr-action]');
30435:       if(!ac)return;
30436:       var page=E('rw-page-container');
30437:       if(page&&page.contains(ac))return;
30438:       e.preventDefault();
30439:       handle(ac.getAttribute('data-hr-action'));
30440:     },true);
30441:     window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',function(e){
30442:       var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
30443:       if(!root)return;
30444:       e.preventDefault();
30445:       var msg=e.reason&&(e.reason.message||String(e.reason));
30446:       if(msg)toast(msg,'error');
30447:     });
30448:     try{
30449:       var mo=new MutationObserver(function(){
30450:         var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
30451:         if(!root||!E('hr360'))return;
30452:         var f=E('rw-hr-form');
30453:         if(f&&f.lastElementChild)f.lastElementChild.style.display='none';
30454:       });
30455:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
30456:     }catch(e){}
30457:   }());
